# Fair Market Rents: Massachusetts

- Postal code: MA
- State FIPS: 25
- Counties: 0
- Towns: 351

HUD's Fair Market Rents for Massachusetts, fiscal years 1983 to 2027: 351 towns, each with its rents by bedroom count and the FMR areas it has sat in. 46 of them have changed FMR area at least once (not counting 2006, when HUD renumbered every area).
